- **brief**: Turn a high-level problem, idea or ticket into a plan the user has signed off on, so build has something concrete to work against. Researches, drafts, grills, writes the plan and its companion, then asks for sign-off. Run as /brief &lt;idea | ticket | problem&gt;. ([zenkoding/skillset](https://github.com/zenkoding/skillset/tree/HEAD/config/skills/base/brief))
- **brief**: How the main session hands a unit to a subagent and what it does when the work comes back wrong. Shape versus fill (who writes what), the model per unit and the `Sonnet because:` line, the brief's template with its unit, criteria and trailers, its checks, the tests brief with its three honesty rules, batches through the Workflow tool, agents running at once, waiting on long runs, the rule for a miss, and who makes each fix after QA. Use before every Agent call that does work (workflow:implementer, a port, tests written to given criteria), and whenever a subagent's report or diff falls short. ([ztsia/workflow](https://github.com/ztsia/workflow/tree/HEAD/plugins/workflow/skills/brief))

- **bright-data-certificate-verify-failed-80d71412042deee6**: Bright Data CERTIFICATE_VERIFY_FAILED behind the proxy - trust the new CA and move to port 44445. Use when hitting this exact issue. Not for unrelated errors or different features.
